# Slurs and vulgar words, all languages. Every wordlist is filtered against
# this file by exact, case-insensitive match. One word per line; edit freely.
#
# Compiled 2026-11-21 from three sources, merged and deduplicated:
#  - the project's original hand-kept English list
#  - List of Dirty, Naughty, Obscene, and Otherwise Bad Words (LDNOOBW),
#    28 languages, CC BY 4.0,
#    https://github.com/LDNOOBW/List-of-Dirty-Naughty-Obscene-and-Otherwise-Bad-Words
#  - words Wiktionary tags as a slur, or mostly vulgar, derogatory, pejorative
#    or offensive, in any language (via Kaikki.org, CC BY-SA)
#
# A word is listed on Wiktionary's evidence only when the flagged senses are at
# least half of its meanings in some language and at least a quarter of its
# meanings across all languages. That keeps "apple" (one slur sense among many)
# and "dog" (a slur in one small language) out of this file. Delete a line to
# allow a word again.
